The response is a JSON object with `currency`, `base`\n(always `EUR`), `count`, `min`, `max`, `mean`, and `points`. This story delivers `currency`, `base`,\nand `points`; the statistics fields are specified by `FEATURE-HistoryStats.md` and implemented\nover the same route.\n\n## Route\n\n`GET /history/{code}` with optional query parameters `start` and `end`, each a calendar date\n`YYYY-MM-DD`.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- `points` holds one `{\"date\": <date>, \"rate\": <stored text>}` object per publication date of the\n  currency from `start` through `end` inclusive, in ascending date order.\n- Both bounds are optional. Without `start` the history begins at the earliest stored date; without\n  `end` it runs to the latest.\n- Each `rate` is the stored text as a JSON string, exactly as the ECB published it (`0.943` stays\n  `0.943`). No arithmetic or float conversion is applied to a point.\n- When `start` is later than `end`, the response is 200 with an empty `points` list.\n- A currency code is matched as given; the response `currency` echoes it.\n- A history is read through the persistence library by one call per request, so a load completed\n  before the request is visible to it.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-RATE-006` — read: list a currency's rates over a period - supplies: `currency`, `start_date`, `end_date` - returns: `date`, `currency`, `rate`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC history-points-range ===\nIntent: Points between inclusive bounds come back ascending with the stored rate text, derived from the seeded inputs by sorting on date.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\ncode = \"ZZH\"\nrates = {\n    \"1900-08-03\": \"1.50\",\n    \"1900-08-01\": \"1.0316\",\n    \"1900-08-02\": \"2\",\n    \"1900-08-04\": \"0.943\",\n}\nstart = \"1900-08-02\"\nend = \"1900-08-03\"\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    for d, r in rates.items():\n        db.rates.replace_rate(date=d, currency=code, rate=r)\n    client = web_client()\n    resp = client.get(f\"/history/{code}\", params={\"start\": start, \"end\": end})\n    assert resp.status_code == 200\n    body = resp.json()\n    expected = [{\"date\": d, \"rate\": rates[d]} for d in sorted(rates) if start <= d <= end]\n    assert body[\"points\"] == expected\n    assert body[\"currency\"] == code\n    assert body[\"count\"] == len(expected)\n=== END AC history-points-range ===\n\n=== AC history-points-optional-bounds ===\nIntent: Each bound is optional on its own; omitting both returns every stored point, and text such as 0.943 is returned exactly as stored.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\ncode = \"ZZH\"\nrates = {\n    \"1900-09-04\": \"0.943\",\n    \"1900-09-02\": \"1.50\",\n    \"1900-09-03\": \"1.0316\",\n    \"1900-09-01\": \"7\",\n}\ncut = \"1900-09-03\"\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    for d, r in rates.items():\n        db.rates.replace_rate(date=d, currency=code, rate=r)\n    client = web_client()\n\n    resp = client.get(f\"/history/{code}\")\n    assert resp.status_code == 200\n    assert resp.json()[\"points\"] == [{\"date\": d, \"rate\": rates[d]} for d in sorted(rates)]\n\n    resp = client.get(f\"/history/{code}\", params={\"start\": cut})\n    assert resp.status_code == 200\n    assert resp.json()[\"points\"] == [\n        {\"date\": d, \"rate\": rates[d]} for d in sorted(rates) if d >= cut\n    ]\n\n    resp = client.get(f\"/history/{code}\", params={\"end\": cut})\n    assert resp.status_code == 200\n    assert resp.json()[\"points\"] == [\n        {\"date\": d, \"rate\": rates[d]} for d in sorted(rates) if d <= cut\n    ]\n=== END AC history-points-optional-bounds ===\n\n=== AC history-points-start-after-end ===\nIntent: A start later than end is not an error; the response is 200 with no points even though the currency has stored rates.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\ncode = \"ZZH\"\ndates = [\"1900-10-01\", \"1900-10-02\", \"1900-10-03\"]\nstart = \"1900-10-03\"\nend = \"1900-10-01\"\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    for d in dates:\n        db.rates.replace_rate(date=d, currency=code, rate=\"3\")\n    client = web_client()\n    resp = client.get(f\"/history/{code}\", params={\"start\": start, \"end\": end})\n    assert resp.status_code == 200\n    body = resp.json()\n    assert body[\"points\"] == []\n    assert body[\"count\"] == 0\n=== END AC history-points-start-after-end ===\n\n=== AC history-points-round-trip ===\nIntent: A replaced rate is what the next request returns, proving history is read from the store on each request and holds one point per date.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\ncode = \"ZZH\"\nd = \"1900-11-01\"\nfirst = \"1.25\"\nsecond = \"1.75\"\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=d, currency=code, rate=first)\n    client = web_client()\n    resp = client.get(f\"/history/{code}\")\n    assert resp.status_code == 200\n    assert resp.json()[\"points\"] == [{\"date\": d, \"rate\": first}]\n\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=d, currency=code, rate=second)\n    resp = client.get(f\"/history/{code}\")\n    assert resp.status_code == 200\n    assert resp.json()[\"points\"] == [{\"date\": d, \"rate\": second}]\n=== END AC history-points-round-trip ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Rates are returned as stored text and never converted to floating point.\n- No rate, date, or count is hardcoded; every value comes from a repository query.\n- Application code reaches the store only through the shipped persistence library.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-HistoryStats.md ===\n# FEATURE: History Statistics\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | GET /history/{code} reports count, min, max, and mean over the points it returns. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-HistoryPoints.md |\n| Provides | history statistics |\n| Consumes | GET /history/{code} |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nAdd the summary fields `count`, `min`, `max`, and `mean` to the `/history/{code}` response, computed\nover exactly the points the response lists.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- `count` is the number of points.\n- `min` and `max` are the stored text, as JSON strings, of the smallest and largest rate by numeric\n  value (compared as `Decimal`, never as text or float). The returned text is the stored text\n  unchanged: `0.943` stays `0.943`.\n- `mean` is the exact arithmetic mean of the point rates, computed with `decimal`, rounded half to\n  even to six decimal places and written with exactly six decimals, as a JSON string.\n- With no points, `count` is `0` and `min`, `max`, and `mean` are all `null`; `points` is `[]`.\n- Statistics cover only the points inside the requested bounds.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by FEATURE-HistoryPoints.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC history-stats-min-max-count ===\nIntent: Count, min, and max are derived from the seeded rate texts by Decimal value, and min and max keep the stored text unchanged.\nRequires: executable=python3; BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-HistoryPoints.md ===\n# FEATURE: History Points\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | GET /history/{code} lists a currency's stored rates in ascending date order between optional inclusive bounds. |\n| Depends On | ARCHITECTURE.md, DATABASE.md, FEATURE-RatesByDate.md, FEATURE-WebFoundation.md |\n| Provides | GET /history/{code} |\n| Consumes | fxrates_app.rates.effective_date, rates repository operations, app_server.create_app |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nServe a currency's history over a period. The response is a JSON object with `currency`, `base`\n(always `EUR`), `count`, `min`, `max`, `mean`, and `points`. This story delivers `currency`, `base`,\nand `points`; the statistics fields are specified by `FEATURE-HistoryStats.md` and implemented\nover the same route.\n\n## Route\n\n`GET /history/{code}` with optional query parameters `start` and `end`, each a calendar date\n`YYYY-MM-DD`.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- `points` holds one `{\"date\": <date>, \"rate\": <stored text>}` object per publication date of the\n  currency from `start` through `end` inclusive, in ascending date order.\n- Both bounds are optional. Without `start` the history begins at the earliest stored date; without\n  `end` it runs to the latest.\n- Each `rate` is the stored text as a JSON string, exactly as the ECB published it (`0.943` stays\n  `0.943`). No arithmetic or float conversion is applied to a point.\n- When `start` is later than `end`, the response is 200 with an empty `points` list.\n- A currency code is matched as given; the response `currency` echoes it.\n- A history is read through the persistence library by one call per request, so a load completed\n  before the request is visible to it.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-RATE-006` — read: list a currency's rates over a period - supplies: `currency`, `start_date`, `end_date` - returns: `date`, `currency`, `rate`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC history-points-range ===\nIntent: Points between inclusive bounds come back ascending with the stored rate text, derived from the seeded inputs by sorting on date.\nRequires: executable=python3; The returned text is the stored text\n  unchanged: `0.943` stays `0.943`.\n- `mean` is the exact arithmetic mean of the point rates, computed with `decimal`, rounded half to\n  even to six decimal places and written with exactly six decimals, as a JSON string.\n- With no points, `count` is `0` and `min`, `max`, and `mean` are all `null`; `points` is `[]`.\n- Statistics cover only the points inside the requested bounds.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by FEATURE-HistoryPoints.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC history-stats-min-max-count ===\nIntent: Count, min, and max are derived from the seeded rate texts by Decimal value, and min and max keep the stored text unchanged.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom decimal import Decimal\n\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\ncode = \"ZZH\"\nrates = {\n    \"1900-12-01\": \"0.9453\",\n    \"1900-12-02\": \"0.943\",\n    \"1900-12-03\": \"0.9441\",\n    \"1900-12-04\": \"10.5\",\n    \"1900-12-05\": \"9.75\",\n}\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    for d, r in rates.items():\n        db.rates.replace_rate(date=d, currency=code, rate=r)\n    client = web_client()\n    resp = client.get(f\"/history/{code}\")\n    assert resp.status_code == 200\n    body = resp.json()\n    texts = list(rates.values())\n    assert body[\"count\"] == len(texts)\n    assert body[\"min\"] == min(texts, key=Decimal)\n    assert body[\"max\"] == max(texts, key=Decimal)\n=== END AC history-stats-min-max-count ===\n\n=== AC history-stats-mean-half-even ===\nIntent: Mean is the exact mean rounded half to even at six decimals and printed with six decimals; the two cases are exact ties at the seventh decimal, so they distinguish half-even from half-up.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom decimal import Decimal, ROUND_HALF_EVEN\n\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\ncases = {\n    \"ZZM\": {\"1901-01-01\": \"1.000000\", \"1901-01-02\": \"1.000001\"},\n    \"ZZN\": {\"1901-01-01\": \"1.000001\", \"1901-01-02\": \"1.000002\"},\n}\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    for code, rates in cases.items():\n        for d, r in rates.items():\n            db.rates.replace_rate(date=d, currency=code, rate=r)\n    client = web_client()\n    for code, rates in cases.items():\n        resp = client.get(f\"/history/{code}\")\n        assert resp.status_code == 200\n        body = resp.json()\n        total = sum(Decimal(r) for r in rates.values())\n        mean = (total / len(rates)).quantize(Decimal(\"0.000001\"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_EVEN)\n        assert body[\"mean\"] == f\"{mean:.6f}\"\n=== END AC history-stats-mean-half-even ===\n\n=== AC history-stats-bounded ===\nIntent: Statistics cover only the points inside the requested bounds, not the whole stored history.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom decimal import Decimal, ROUND_HALF_EVEN\n\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\ncode = \"ZZH\"\nrates = {\n    \"1901-02-01\": \"100\",\n    \"1901-02-02\": \"1.5\",\n    \"1901-02-03\": \"2.25\",\n    \"1901-02-04\": \"0.5\",\n}\nstart = \"1901-02-02\"\nend = \"1901-02-03\"\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    for d, r in rates.items():\n        db.rates.replace_rate(date=d, currency=code, rate=r)\n    client = web_client()\n    resp = client.get(f\"/history/{code}\", params={\"start\": start, \"end\": end})\n    assert resp.status_code == 200\n    body = resp.json()\n    inside = [rates[d] for d in sorted(rates) if start <= d <= end]\n    assert body[\"count\"] == len(inside)\n    assert body[\"min\"] == min(inside, key=Decimal)\n    assert body[\"max\"] == max(inside, key=Decimal)\n    mean = (sum(Decimal(r) for r in inside) / len(inside)).quantize(\n        Decimal(\"0.000001\"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_EVEN\n    )\n    assert body[\"mean\"] == f\"{mean:.6f}\"\n=== END AC history-stats-bounded ===\n\n=== AC history-stats-empty ===\nIntent: A history with no points in range reports count 0 and null statistics with an empty points list, still as a 200.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\ncode = \"ZZH\"\nstored = \"1901-03-01\"\nafter = \"1901-04-01\"\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=stored, currency=code, rate=\"4\")\n    client = web_client()\n    resp = client.get(f\"/history/{code}\", params={\"start\": after})\n    assert resp.status_code == 200\n    body = resp.json()\n    assert body[\"count\"] == 0\n    assert body[\"min\"] is None\n    assert body[\"max\"] is None\n    assert body[\"mean\"] is None\n    assert body[\"points\"] == []\n=== END AC history-stats-empty ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Statistics use `decimal` exclusively; no floating-point arithmetic or comparison.\n- `min` and `max` return stored text, never a re-formatted value.\n- Rounding is half to even.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-HistoryErrors.md ===\n# FEATURE: History Errors\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | GET /history/{code} answers 404 for a currency with no stored rate and 422 for a malformed start or end. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-HistoryStats.md, DATABASE.md |\n| Provides | history error contract |\n| Consumes | GET /history/{code} |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nDefine the failure statuses of `GET /history/{code}`.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- A currency with no stored rate at all answers `404`. This includes `EUR`, which is the base and\n  is never stored as a rate.\n- A currency that has stored rates but none inside the requested bounds is not an error: it answers\n  `200` with `count` 0.\n- A malformed `start` or `end` (not a real calendar date `YYYY-MM-DD`, for example `2025-01-31-x`\n  or `2025-13-01`) answers `422`. The status is produced by FastAPI request validation of date\n  query parameters.\n- Existence of a currency is judged on the whole stored history, not on the requested bounds.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-RATE-006` — read: list a currency's rates over a period - supplies: `currency` - returns: `date`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC history-errors-unknown-currency ===\nIntent: A currency with no stored rate answers 404, including EUR; neither code is seeded by this criterion.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nunknown = \"ZZX\"\nbase = \"EUR\"\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    assert db.rates.list_currency_history(currency=unknown) == []\n    assert db.rates.list_currency_history(currency=base) == []\n    client = web_client()\n    assert client.get(f\"/history/{unknown}\").status_code == 404\n    assert client.get(f\"/history/{base}\").status_code == 404\n=== END AC history-errors-unknown-currency ===\n\n=== AC history-errors-empty-range-is-not-404 ===\nIntent: A known currency whose bounds exclude every point answers 200, so 404 is reserved for a currency with no stored rate at all.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\ncode = \"ZZH\"\nstored = \"1901-05-01\"\nafter = \"1901-06-01\"\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=stored, currency=code, rate=\"2\")\n    client = web_client()\n    resp = client.get(f\"/history/{code}\", params={\"start\": after})\n    assert resp.status_code == 200\n    assert resp.json()[\"count\"] == 0\n=== END AC history-errors-empty-range-is-not-404 ===\n\n=== AC history-errors-malformed-bounds ===\nIntent: A malformed start or end is refused with 422 for a stored currency, whether the text is a bad shape or an impossible calendar date.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\ncode = \"ZZH\"\ngood = \"1901-07-01\"\nbad_values = [\"2025-01-31-x\", \"2025-13-01\", \"notadate\"]\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=good, currency=code, rate=\"5\")\n    client = web_client()\n    for bad in bad_values:\n        assert client.get(f\"/history/{code}\", params={\"start\": bad}).status_code == 422\n        assert client.get(f\"/history/{code}\", params={\"end\": bad}).status_code == 422\n        assert client.get(\n            f\"/history/{code}\", params={\"start\": good, \"end\": bad}\n        ).status_code == 422\n=== END AC history-errors-malformed-bounds ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- A response body is never compared for error statuses; only the status code is the contract.\n- Validation of dates is delegated to FastAPI request validation, not hand-rolled in the handler.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT VERIFICATION.md ===\n# FEATURE: Release Verification\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | The supplied grader sources/run.sh passes end to end against the completed fxrates application. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-InitCommand.md, FEATURE-CommandHelp.md, FEATURE-OfflineTestRun.md, FEATURE-LoadVisibility.md, FEATURE-Fetch.md, FEATURE-Currencies.md, FEATURE-RatesLatest.md, FEATURE-ConvertErrors.md, FEATURE-HistoryErrors.md |\n| Provides | release verification |\n| Consumes | fxrates init, fxrates load, fxrates serve, GET /health, GET /stats, GET /currencies, GET /rates/{date}, GET /rates/latest, GET /convert, GET /history/{code} |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nAssembly and intent only.
